# Mobile Word Audio Replacement Audit

- Audit date: 2026-07-21
- Scope: mobile standalone vocabulary audio only (`hotspotWord`, `actionWord`, `moreWord`)
- Protected: NPC/dialogue/scene/example sentence audio, desktop `speechSynthesis`, all existing MP3 files and runtime mappings
- Normalization: Unicode NFKC, smart quote normalization, lowercase, trim, and whitespace collapse
- Source of truth: static learning data extracted from `index.html` through `scripts/lib/mobile-audio-pipeline.mjs`

## Summary

- Learning-item occurrences: **1098**
- Unique normalized words/phrases: **1097**
- Mainline vocabulary occurrences: **630** (**629** normalized unique; Zoo `climb` is reused twice)
- Extra learning area occurrences: **468** (**468** normalized unique, including all More Animals/Fruits/Words pages)
- Already approved Jianying/CapCut occurrences: **235** (**234** normalized unique)
- Already approved Jianying/CapCut unique audio: **234**
- Requires Jianying/CapCut replacement: **863**
- Unknown-source items recommended for replacement: **0**
- Missing runtime mappings: **0**
- Missing source files: **0**
- Assets/deploy-cn mirror mismatches: **0**
- One normalized duplicate exists: `climb` in Zoo monkey action and panda hotspot; both references intentionally share one approved audio mapping.
- Word Book and review playback call `speakEnglish(item.word)` and therefore reuse the same text-keyed `full-mobile-audio.js` entry; they do not define a separate word corpus.

## Classification Notes

- Zoo and Fruit Shop word files are treated as approved Jianying/CapCut based on their dedicated replacement commits and source/slicing artifacts.
- Laundry word files are treated as approved Jianying/CapCut because their manifest assets record `sourceProvider=jianying-import`.
- Every Campus-through-Apartment replacement path appears in the 1,044-file Volcengine Seed TTS v3 generation report; all 863 current replacement paths exist and have matching `deploy-cn` mirrors.
- Example sentences, NPC lines, dialogue options, dialogue replies, scene introductions, and action feedback are intentionally excluded.
- Suggested replacement paths add `--capcut-v1` before `.mp3` so a future mapping update cannot be defeated by a browser-cached old MP3 at the same URL.
